View Test Case
Preconditions
Staging environment (STAG_ENV baseUrl) selected. Collection variables cleared.
Steps
  1. 1. POST V1 Search (api/FlightSearch/Search) with the above body. 2. POST V2 Search (api/V2/FlightSearch/Search) with the same criteria. 3. Record offers count and canBeHeld per offer.
Expected Result
HTTP 200 on both versions with non-empty offers[]; no 'credentials ... do not exist or are not active' error. Record canBeHeld (needed for Family E cases).
Actual Result
Both versions 200 with 19 offers (Qatar Airways via DOH); canBeHeld=true on every offer, so the Hold-based cases (Family E) can run
TC-CAPCHECK-SABRENDC-02 - [Response] Verify a SabreNdc Book returns ticket numbers and a voidCapability value indicating Void is allowed.FAIL
Endpoint Name: Book a SabreNdc flight and check it shows whether it can be voided
Request: Booked one adult Cairo to Jeddah on 12 Nov 2026 on each API version, then looked the booking up
Response: Both bookings were ticketed at once (V1 TFZFYS, V2 TKTQQW), but neither the booking nor the lookup says whether a void is allowed
Expected: The booking should be ticketed and should carry a flag saying the void is allowed
Actual: Failed: the tickets are issued, but the void-eligibility flag (voidCapability) is missing on both versions
Status: FAIL
Evidence
V1 Book keys: tickets,creationDate,ticketingDate,holdExpirationDate,supplier,pnr,gdsPNR,bookingToken,journeys,...,diRemarks (no voidCapability). V2 Book keys: supplier,pnr,gdsPNR,bookingToken,creationDate,ticketingDate,tickets,...,emdTickets (no voidCapability). Retrieve also lacks it on both; V2 Retrieve has bookingStatus Booked, V1 Retrieve has none.

Preconditions
TC-CAPCHECK-SABRENDC-01 passed. Staging only.
Steps
  1. 1. Search -> FareConfirm -> Book (V1) / Search -> FareConfirm -> AddPassenger -> Book (V2). 2. Record pnr, gdsPNR, bookingToken, tickets[] (ticket numbers, ticketStatus), voidCapability, ticketingDate.
Expected Result
HTTP 200; pnr, gdsPNR, bookingToken non-empty; tickets[] populated (or populated by an immediate Retrieve if ticketing is async, §2.9). Record the exact voidCapability enum value seen for a freshly ticketed SabreNdc booking as the 'void allowed' baseline (only VoidNotAllowed is known today).
Actual Result
Tickets are issued synchronously on both versions (ticketingDate 2026-10-22), but voidCapability is missing from Book and Retrieve on V1 and V2, so a caller cannot tell whether Void is allowed

View Test Case
Preconditions
Void succeeded.
Steps
  1. 1. POST Retrieve (V1 RetrieveBooking / V2 Retrieve) immediately after Void. 2. Record status, tickets[].ticketStatus, voidCapability, and any status field.
Expected Result
Booking shows as voided: e.g. tickets[].ticketStatus changed from Booked to a voided value, voidCapability no longer allows void - or a terminal 409 like cancelled PNRs. Exact signal UNKNOWN (bookingStatus does not exist in V1 Retrieve, §2.11) - record it as the baseline. Unchanged Booked tickets = defect.
Actual Result
Retrieve no longer returns the booking; it answers with a terminal "This Trip Is Already Cancelled" on both versions. Baseline recorded. Note the HTTP 400 / generic invalid-request code differs from the 409 NDC-INT-BYP-A0003 used for cancelled holds (see TC-VOID-NEG-05)

View Test Case
Preconditions
Booking created and ticketed on day D (record ticketingDate and time zone); Void deliberately not called until after midnight.
Steps
  1. 1. Day D: Book and record ticketingDate. 2. Day D+1: Retrieve and record voidCapability. 3. POST Void. 4. Retrieve again.
Expected Result
Void rejected with a business-rule error in the standard envelope (exact HTTP status/ErrorCode UNKNOWN - record); booking stays ticketed. voidCapability at step 2 should already indicate void not allowed (e.g. VoidNotAllowed).
Actual Result
Blocked: this single-session run cannot cross midnight. No booking was left for it (it would stay ticketed and unvoidable)

Preconditions
Hold succeeded (tickets[] empty, holdExpirationDate set).
Steps
  1. 1. Search -> FareConfirm -> (V2: AddPassenger ->) Hold. 2. POST Void. 3. Retrieve. 4. Clean up with CancelAfterHold.

View Test Case
Preconditions

TC-VOID-VAL-04 - [Request] Validate Void with a surName that does not match any passenger on the PNR is rejected.FAIL
Endpoint Name: Void using a surname that is not on the booking

Response: Both were accepted and the tickets were voided

Actual: Failed: the surname is not checked, so anyone with the booking reference and token can void it
Status: FAIL

View Test Case
Preconditions
Those cases executed.
Steps
  1. 1. Tabulate HTTP status, Status, ErrorCode, ErrorMessage, Context per failure. 2. Compare.
Expected Result
Every failure uses {Status, TransactionId, ErrorCode, ErrorMessage, Context}; Status equals HTTP code; validation errors are 400; business-rule/eligibility errors use one consistent code family. Record the full mapping table for SPEC.md §2.15.
Actual Result
Two different envelope casings are used (PascalCase vs camelCase with errorCode "1001" and extra originalSupplier* keys); business-rule rejections share the generic validation code and an "invalid request parameters" message; not-found is an unmapped 409 catch-all. V1 and V2 behave the same

Findings8Defects0Unknown-resolution
Defect
SabreNdc Book and Retrieve responses omit voidCapability on V1 and V2, even for a booking ticketed today (§2.9 voidCapability; §2.11; §4.7, TC-CAPCHECK-SABRENDC-02)
Field: voidCapability
Expected value: "voidCapability": "<void-allowed value>" on a same-day ticketed booking (e.g. VoidAllowed), switching to VoidNotAllowed once voided or after the void window
Impact: Callers cannot decide from the booking whether to offer Void; the capability-flag consistency check (TC-VOID-BIZ-05) cannot be performed
Evidence: V1 Book TFZFYS and V2 Book TKTQQW (2026-10-22): key lists contain no voidCapability; same for RetrieveBooking/Retrieve
Defect
A second Void on an already-voided SabreNdc booking returns 200 voided:true instead of an already-voided rejection (V1 and V2) (§2.15 Negative Cases, TC-VOID-BIZ-02)
Field: voided
Expected value: HTTP 409 (or 4xx) standard envelope, e.g. {Status:409, ErrorCode:"AlreadyVoided", ErrorMessage:"Tickets on this booking are already voided"}
Impact: Callers cannot tell a real void from a repeat; a retry looks like a fresh success and the response contradicts Retrieve, which says the trip is cancelled
Evidence: TFZFYS (V1) and TKTQQW (V2): first Void 200 in ~10s, Retrieve 400 This Trip Is Already Cancelled, second Void 200 voided:true in ~1.5s
Defect
SabreNdc Void ignores surName: a surname that matches no passenger (or no surname at all) voids the booking (V1 and V2) (§2.15 Common Errors, TC-VOID-VAL-04)
Field: surName
Expected value: 4xx standard envelope, e.g. {Status:400, ErrorCode:"NDC-INT-TEC-A0001", Context:[{Value:"surName does not match any passenger on the PNR"}]}, booking left ticketed
Impact: The passenger-identity check documented for Void does not exist; a caller holding only pnr + bookingToken can void any ticketed SabreNdc booking (also lets every SEC-04 injection string through)
Evidence: OOLKNB (V1) and TMNYPF (V2) voided with surName 'Mismatch'; OPWPBD (V1) and OXXGBK (V2) voided with surName omitted; 2026-10-22
Defect
Void with voidTickets naming a ticket that is not on the PNR voids every ticket on the PNR instead of being rejected (V1 and V2) (§2.15 voidTickets, TC-VOID-NEG-03)
Field: voidTickets
Expected value: 4xx standard envelope, e.g. Context [{Value:"Ticket 0650000000000 is not on PNR TLPJBW"}], tickets unchanged
Impact: Selective void silently becomes void-all: a caller asking to void one (wrong) ticket loses every ticket on the booking
Evidence: TLPJBW (V1) ticket 1572135118447 and OWVBLV (V2) ticket 1572135118446 voided by voidTickets [{ticketNumber:0650000000000}], 2026-10-22
Defect
Void deserialization error leaks the internal .NET type name NDC.Integrations.Application.Dtos.Api.SharedTypes.TicketType (and a stray "voidRequestDto field is required" entry) (§2.15 voidTickets, TC-VOID-NEG-03)
Field: context[].value
Expected value: context [{name:"voidTickets[0]", value:"Each voidTickets item must be an object with ticketNumber"}]
Impact: Internal type names exposed to callers; known DTO-leak defect now also on Void
Evidence: voidTickets ['0650000000000'] -> 400 errorCode 1001 on V1 and V2
Defect
After CancelAfterHold, SabreNdc Retrieve still returns 200 for the cancelled hold; V2 reports bookingStatus OnHold (total 0, holdExpirationDate null), V1 shows no cancelled signal (§2.14; §2.15, TC-VOID-BIZ-04)
Field: bookingStatus
Expected value: A terminal signal, e.g. bookingStatus "Cancelled" or the documented 409 NDC-INT-BYP-A0003 "Reservation is already cancelled"
Impact: Callers polling Retrieve cannot tell a cancelled hold from a live one
Evidence: OXXYGW (V1) and TFHFMB (V2): CancelAfterHold 200 {cancelled:true, message:"Held Order Is Cancelled"}, then Retrieve 200 with OnHold (V2)
Defect
Void for an unknown SabreNdc order returns the unmapped 409 NDC-INT-UNC-Z9999 (message = the code) and echoes the raw Sabre request and response (§2.15; §7.9, TC-VOID-NEG-01)
Field: ErrorCode / OriginalSupplierResponse
Expected value: {Status:404 or 409, ErrorCode:"BookingNotFound", ErrorMessage:"No booking found for the supplied PNR / booking token", Context:[...]} with no OriginalSupplier* fields
Impact: Callers get no usable error, and supplier internals are exposed in the API response
Evidence: V1 TransactionId 903b824c-..., V2 f42c2c1d-..., 2026-10-22
Defect
Void errors use two envelope casings (Status/ErrorCode vs status/errorCode "1001" + originalSupplier* keys), and business-rule rejections reuse the generic "Invalid request" validation code (§2.15; §2.9 Expected Failure, TC-VOID-NEG-05)
Field: error envelope
Expected value: {Status, TransactionId, ErrorCode, ErrorMessage, Context} everywhere; a distinct business-rule code (e.g. VoidNotEligible) for held/cancelled/mismatched bookings
Impact: Clients need two parsers and cannot tell a bad request from an ineligible booking
Evidence: Validation (missing pnr/bookingToken): 400 PascalCase {Status, TransactionId, ErrorCode:NDC-INT-TEC-A0001}. Validation (missing supplier/credentialsSelector, bad voidTickets item): 400 camelCase {status, transactionId, errorCode:"1001", context, originalSupplierRequest:null, originalSupplierResponse:null}. Inactive selector: 400 NDC-INT-TEC-A0001. Business rules (token/PNR mismatch, held not ticketed, cancelled hold, Retrieve of voided trip): 400 NDC-INT-TEC-A0001 "Invalid request. Please check the request parameters". Not found: 409 NDC-INT-UNC-Z9999 with raw Sabre payload. Wrong supplier: 409 OperationNotSupported. Auth: 401 AuthenticationError. Status always equals the HTTP code; TransactionId always a GUID.

Also re-confirmed this round: surName not validated (Same root cause as TC-VOID-VAL-04: every injection surname voided the booking) — see TC-VOID-SEC-04; voidCapability missing (Same defect as TC-CAPCHECK-SABRENDC-02, seen on all 26 SabreNdc bookings/holds of this run, including BookAfterHold) — see TC-VOID-BIZ-05.

Proposed SPEC.md updates5Proposed Updates

§2.15 Expected Success Response — status: pending

Old: "**Expected Success Response**: `UNKNOWN` for a void-eligible booking — not observed this run."

New: "**Expected Success Response** `[Confirmed live 2026-10-22, SabreNdc/EGY, V1+V2]`: HTTP 200 `{voided: true, voidedTickets: [{ticketStatus: "Voided", ticketNumber, carrierCode}]}`; V2 additionally returns `passengersVoidedAncillaries: {}` and `emdTickets: {}`. Ticketing is synchronous at Book for SabreNdc, so a same-day Void can follow Book immediately (~10s per Void)."

§2.15 Required Request Fields (voidTickets, gdsPNR) — status: pending

Old: "`voidTickets` (array, empty `[]` in every saved example — `UNKNOWN` exact shape when populated, presumably ticket numbers to selectively void vs. void-all-when-empty)."

New: "`voidTickets` `[Confirmed live 2026-10-22, SabreNdc]`: `[]` voids every ticket on the PNR (ADT+CHD+INF confirmed); each element is a TicketType object `{ticketNumber, carrierCode, ticketStatus}` (a plain string fails deserialization with 400 errorCode 1001). DEFECT: an element naming a ticket not on the PNR is ignored and all tickets are voided. `gdsPNR` is not required for SabreNdc — `""` is accepted and the void succeeds."

§2.15 Common Errors — status: pending

Old: "**Common Errors**: voiding outside the void window; voiding a booking whose `voidCapability` was already `"VoidNotAllowed"` at Book time; voiding with an incorrect `surName`."

New: "**Common Errors** `[Confirmed live 2026-10-22, SabreNdc]`: held/unticketed or cancelled hold → 400 NDC-INT-TEC-A0001 "Only fulfilled, ticketed orders can be voided."; pnr/bookingToken from different orders → 400 "PNR does not match the supplied Sabre order ID."; missing pnr / bookingToken → 400 NotEmptyValidator; unknown order → 409 NDC-INT-UNC-Z9999 (unmapped, defect); a repeat Void returns 200 voided:true (defect); `surName` is NOT checked — a wrong or missing surname still voids (defect). Retrieve after a Void returns 400 NDC-INT-TEC-A0001 "This Trip Is Already Cancelled"."

§2.9 Expected Success Response (voidCapability) — status: pending

Old: "voidCapability, // e.g. "VoidNotAllowed" — INFERRED a live, booking-specific flag indicating whether Void is currently possible for this PNR (see §2.14)"

New: "voidCapability, // e.g. "VoidNotAllowed" — documented, but [Confirmed live 2026-10-22] NOT returned by SabreNdc (V1/V2 Book, BookAfterHold, Retrieve) nor by TravelportNdc Book; tracked as a defect"

§4.6 Capability Summary Matrix (SabreNDC row) — status: pending

Old: "| SabreNDC | Unknown | Unknown | Supported | Supported | Unknown | Unknown | Unknown |"

New: "| SabreNDC | Unknown | Not Supported (haveBundles false on all offers, EGY 2026-10-22) | Supported | Supported | Supported (EGY, same-day, V1+V2, confirmed 2026-10-22) | Unknown | Unknown |"
